> [!warning] Conflict
> Flagged while digesting this document, not verified: 4RF converts purchase requisitions with the app labelled Process Purchase Requisitions - Deprecated (F1048), while 4VT, 4WM, 54U and 54V use Process Purchase Requisitions (F1048A).
